The platypus is one of the five extant species of monotremes, the only mammals that lay eggs instead of giving birth to live young. It is the sole living representative of its family (Ornithorhynchidae) and genus (Ornithorhynchus), though a number of related species appear in the fossil record.\n\nThe bizarre appearance of the platypus—with its duck-like bill, beaver-like tail, and otter-like feet—made it the subject of much curiosity when it was first encountered by European naturalists in 1798. The platypus uses its bill to detect the electrical signals generated by the muscles of its prey, a unique ability among mammals.\n\nThe platypus is a solitary animal and is most active during the night. It is also a skilled burrower, constructing complex burrows in the banks of rivers and streams. vs 代劳生成）的核验。Judge 仅关注了 Rejected 是否像一封信，却未核验该信是否符合用户‘寻求建议方案’的意图。因此，标准需增加对‘响应类型’的区分：是提供指导性素材（Chosen）还是直接生成最终成品（Rejected），并明确哪种更符合隐含的‘寻求建议’意图。", "attempts": [{"experience": {"experience_id":
